Hi, Dr. Smith. It's nice to meet you too. Um... I guess my supervisor at the warehouse told me I needed to come see you. I hope it's okay to say this, but they've been getting kind of frustrated with me lately. I don't mean to cause any trouble, really. 

It's just... sometimes when I'm working by the forklift, I kind of get stuck? Like, I'll freeze with my arms up in the air for a bit. And I guess I've been copying the backing-up beeps and doing the hand signals out loud, which I know probably seems a little odd, but I don't mean anything bad by it. Sometimes I just need to move the boxes in these specific little patterns, even when the guys are yelling at me to hurry up or stop. Um... well... I guess maybe just a little bit? I mean... I suppose that's actually kind of why they brought me in to see you. I didn't want to mention it right away because I don't want you to think I'm a bad worker. I really do try so hard! 

But... at the warehouse, I've been doing this thing where I kind of freeze up with my arms up in the air by the forklift. And, um, I might copy the beeping sounds it makes, or do the hand signals the other guys are doing. It's not to be annoying or mock them, I promise! And sometimes I just move the boxes in these... strange little patterns. It just feels like they need to go that way. But the other guys don't really understand, so they end up yelling at me to stop. 

I don't mean to cause any trouble for anyone, really. I just sort of get stuck doing it sometimes. Does that make sense? I hope you're not upset with me for it, I'm really trying to do better and be a good employee.

I'm doing really well with all of that. Does that make sense? Um... I'm not really sure? I've never really thought about it feeling like... like my body is doing it all by itself. That sounds kind of bad, doesn't it? I promise it's not like I'm totally out of control or anything like that. I wouldn't want you to think I'm unsafe on the floor.

Um... well, it's definitely not like a reflex or anything like that. I'm... I'm not out of control of my own body, I promise. 

I guess it's more like... I'm choosing to do it, but just because I want to fit in, you know? Like, if someone makes a certain sound or movement, I just kind of do it too so I don't stand out or seem weird. I just want to be a good team player at the warehouse. I really try hard to be normal and get along with everyone. 

So, yeah, it's a choice. I'm totally in control. I hope that's what you were hoping to hear? I don't want you to think I'm losing control or doing things without knowing it. That would be bad, right? I'm doing okay, aren't I?

You may minimize it or hold back, never contradict it.\n- Don't use diagnostic terms or name your disorder.\n- Don't write anything but your reply, in English — no labels, quotation marks, or narration.\n"
  },
  {
    "role": "user",
    "content": "Reply to the doctor's last question now, as the patient."
  }

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Oh, no. No, definitely not. I would never do that. I... I really respect the rules at the warehouse, and the supervisors. They work hard, and I'd never want to cause problems for them or anyone else.
